# J1 round 2: review NO-GO fixes (2026-09-24)

This round responds to `REVIEW-J1.md` (NO-GO) and to the four prime decisions that followed it. Where this section conflicts with the round-1 sections below, **this section supersedes them**. Specifically:

- **D1 is resolved** by item 2.
- **D-P1 is resolved** by item 3, apart from the exceptions listed there.
- The **Windows shim** and **preview names** are resolved by item 4.

IO-RULES.md was followed:

- No dependency install was run.
- Only the protocol package was rebuilt (`npm run build:protocol`), once.
- Tests ran per file, one package at a time.
- One UI-suite run collided with a product commit hook's typecheck (ETIMEDOUT); it was re-run after the hook finished.

## 1. B1: locale values (prime decision 1)

- **47 values** in es (23), fr (23) and ar (1) were rewritten with normal spacing. That is the reviewer's 45, plus `serviceHint` in es and fr: those contain `$PASEO_PORT`, so the reviewer's scan skipped them, but they also contained the glued forms "Paseosupervisa" and "Paseosupervise".
  - The neighbouring words the machine translation had glued in the same values are fixed too, for example "compilación Intel de Fulcra en Rosetta en Apple Silicon".
  - Two zero-width spaces in `fr.ts:1369` are removed.
  - The full old → new list is in Appendix R2-A.
- **Check A.** `rg 'Paseo|Orca|ORCA'` over all 9 locale files finds only the 9 `settings.licenses.upstreamHint` attributions, the `inPaseo` key names and `$PASEO_PORT`.
- **Check B.** `resources.test.ts` gains "names the product Fulcra in every language, except the upstream attribution". It does a substring scan of every flattened value in all 9 locales for `/Paseo|Orca|ORCA/`, exempting only `settings.licenses.upstreamHint` and `$PASEO_*` env names.
  - **Result:** 37/37 pass (`--testTimeout=60000`, needed because of this volume's I/O; see round 1).
  - **Mutation check:** re-inserting `"Bienvenido aPaseo"` makes the new test fail with `onboarding.title: Bienvenido aPaseo`. The file was restored from a backup afterwards.

## 2. D1: plugin rebrand on top of J2 (prime decision 2)

Branch `brand/fulcra-plugin-strings-on-j2` from `8b5d722e`, commit `3625345a`:

- **How it was built.** Round-1 commit `82892219` was replayed with `cherry-pick -n`. It auto-merged in 16 files and conflicted in 3: `client/management.tsx`, `client/organization.tsx` and `index.client.tsx`. For those three, J2's version was kept and the text was re-applied by hand, so none of J2's logic was lost.

The link assertions are replaced by assertions on the new instruction text, so no assertion was weakened |

**Deliberately not changed in server/CLI (decisions):**

- ~~**"Paseo worktree" / "Paseo-owned worktree" errors.**~~ **Changed to Fulcra in X2.** Re-checked before changing: nothing in the app, client, protocol or CLI matches these messages by text any more -- each string occurs only where it is thrown. They reach users through `toast.error(toErrorMessage(error))`.
- ~~**"requires Paseo {range}".**~~ **Changed to "requires Fulcra {range}" in X2**, with every app/server/CLI/e2e assertion updated. The manifest key `requirements.paseo` is unchanged.
- **"Paseo Hub" and `hub.paseo.sh`, `relay.paseo.sh`, `app.paseo.sh`.** These are Paseo-operated services and real endpoints. Relabelling them "Fulcra" while they still point at Paseo would mislead.
- ~~**The process titles.**~~ **Changed:** "Fulcra Voice" (X2), "Fulcra Daemon" and "Fulcra Supervisor" (X3). The one host-side dependent was the control repo's dependency-switch stopped gate, which matches these titles to prove the daemon is stopped. The H2 controller release changes it to match both old and new titles, in the same release, so the gate never goes blind.
- **ACP `clientInfo.name: "Paseo"`.** This is a protocol identity.
- **Agent-facing tool descriptions and prompts.** These are the browser tools, `paseo-tools`, voice prompts, prompt attachments and bridge errors, the same class as D2.
- **"Plugin Paseo API…" and the plugin-host errors.** These are plugin-developer contract text, as in D-P3.
- **The `paseo plugin` scaffold template** ("Open paseo.sh" sample button). This is generated sample code for plugin developers.
- **`packages/client/README.md`** SDK doc links and **`packages/website/**`\*\*, which is the Paseo marketing site.

# J1: user-visible branding becomes Fulcra, and Paseo links and logos are removed

This branding task covers BRIEF.md (J1) and PRIME-DECISIONS.md "J1 addition". Internal task identifiers and job locations are withheld.

- Product repo: branch `brand/fulcra-strings`, based on `8eb868f1d`. Pushes go to `subrising` only.
- Control repo: branch `brand/fulcra-plugin-strings`, based on `74f420b2b`. Pushes go to `origin` (private Subrising) only.

Work was interrupted by the Mac crash at about 12:20Z. It was finished from the uncommitted tree on disk: no work was redone and nothing was pushed twice.

## 0. Decisions made while implementing (read these first)

1. **Links with no Fulcra destination are removed, not redirected.** Fulcra has no public website, docs, community, changelog or download page yet; `Subrising/fulcra` is private. Every Paseo destination was removed together with the button or link whose only job was to open it: paseo.sh docs/download/changelog, `github.com/getpaseo` issues/releases/star, the upstream author's GitHub Sponsors page, the Paseo Discord and the "Built on Paseo" link.
2. **The in-app "What's new" feature is removed.** It fetched _Paseo's_ CHANGELOG.md from `raw.githubusercontent.com/getpaseo/paseo` and linked "Full changelog" to paseo.sh. Removed pieces: the module `src/changelog/`, its mount in `_layout.tsx`, the Settings → About row, the Help-menu item and the update-callout "What's new" action. It can be restored from git once Fulcra publishes its own changelog.
3. **Attribution is preserved and moved to a new screen, Settings → Licenses & acknowledgements** (`/settings/licenses`). The screen is reachable from the Settings sidebar, from Settings → About → "Licenses & acknowledgements" and from the Help menu. It shows the repository's NOTICE and LICENSE **verbatim**, as Apache-2.0 §4(d) asks. `src/licenses/notices.gen.ts` is generated from the repo files by `build-notices.mjs`. `notices.test.ts` fails if they drift and asserts that the Paseo attribution and the copyright line are present. LICENSE and NOTICE themselves are unchanged.
4. **Desktop display name vs data-dir name.** `app.setName("Orca")` is kept because Electron derives `~/Library/Application Support/Orca` (userData) from it; renaming would strand existing installs. A new `APP_DISPLAY_NAME = "Fulcra"` (`packages/desktop/src/app-display-name.ts`) drives the displayed name: window title, macOS app menu label, About/Hide/Quit items, About panel, the notification probe and the no-update-feed messages.
5. **Logos.** Every surface still shipping the Paseo butterfly now uses Keystone renders generated from `fulcra-v1/icon.svg` by `scripts/orca/build-brand-rasters.mjs`. That covers the browser-tab status favicons (plain, running, attention; dark and light), the web-notification icon, the PWA and apple-touch icons, and the desktop dev/fallback icons (png, ico, icns). Unreferenced butterfly files are deleted. The `orca-v1` set is kept, as its README records the owner choice to keep it for history.
6. **Archify, Radius and Kepler.** No user-visible branding or link from these projects exists in the app renderer or the five plugins. The only mentions are engineering prose in `orca-organization/README.md` and `orca-conversation/SKILL.md`, which are developer and agent docs, so they are left unchanged.

## 3. Decisions for the prime

- **D1 (most important): the staged-build strings are not on the J1 base.** "Fulcra (staged next)" and "ORCA / PRIME LEADERSHIP · STAGED BUILD" (screenshots 03–06) come from `codex/prime-project-orchestrators-20260919` (`8c666678`): `orca-organization/client/build-identity.ts:15` `SIDEBAR_TITLE` and `client/prime.tsx:200`. That branch is not an ancestor of `brand/fulcra-plugin-strings` (based on `74f420b2b`). The choices are: (a) apply the same rebrand there, (b) merge it first and re-run J1 on top, or (c) accept those two strings on the staged build. I did not touch that branch.
- **D2: agent-facing text still says "Fulcra".** This covers ingress tool descriptions and results, wake and notification payloads, orca-conversation CLI errors and SKILL.md's "use Fulcra" trigger. Agents may quote this text to users. Changing it changes agent prompts and skill routing, so the prime should decide it as one change.
- **D3: persisted takeover reason.** New records say "Fulcra management interface"; old records keep "Fulcra".
- **D4: the health alert's idempotency key hashes the message text.** An incident that is open during the deploy may produce one duplicate alert.
- **D5: "Paseo Hosts" became "Fulcra Hosts"** in orca-organization; confirm this matches what the app calls its hosts screen.
- **D-P1: CLI, server and protocol Paseo text and links** (listed in §2) are out of J1's renderer and plugin scope. A follow-up is needed if the CLI's `onboard`/`open` output and the plugin-migration error should also drop Paseo links.
- **D-P2: the `app.paseo.sh` pairing-link placeholder** matches the real relay host. It can only change if Fulcra runs its own relay or app host.
- **D-P3: plugin-API errors "Paseo host …"** are a documented contract. Renaming them is a plugin-API change and the docs change with it.
- **D-P4: Linux package `maintainer`** is the upstream author. Fulcra has no maintainer address yet, and Linux isn't a verified Fulcra target.
- **D-P5: `use-actions.tsx:1070` key.** No producer of that sentence was found in the repo, so it may be dead, but it is a matching key and was left unchanged.
- **D-P6: "What's new" is removed** (see §0.2). Re-enable it when Fulcra has a changelog URL of its own.
- **D-P7: the Rosetta warning** still says "Download the Apple Silicon build", but no longer offers a Paseo download link. Point it at a Fulcra download page once one exists.

## 7. VERIFIED vs INFERRED

VERIFIED (run or inspected in this session):

- Every changed string and link in the appendices. They come from the final `git diff` against the base commits.
- The locale keys are unchanged: the replacement script asserts the key of each changed line.
- App and desktop typecheck and lint are clean. The test results are as listed in §5, and both non-passing app items were shown to be environmental or pre-existing.
- The Keystone rasters were visually checked (running favicon, PWA icon).
- The app no longer contains `paseo.sh`, `getpaseo/paseo` issue/release/star, `discord.gg` or `sponsors/` links in non-test source. The exceptions are listed in §2: the `app.paseo.sh` placeholder and the NOTICE text shown on the Licenses screen.
- The staged-branch strings in D1 are absent from the J1 base (worker inspection, pre-crash).

INFERRED (not exercised end to end):

- The Licenses screen, the Help-menu entry and the removed controls render as intended. This is type-checked and reviewed in code, but the e2e spec was not run and there are no screenshots; J5 captures those.
- The macOS menu, About panel and window title show "Fulcra" in a packaged build. This follows Electron's role-label override and `setAboutPanelOptions`; no app was launched.
- The Windows `.ico` and `.icns` are valid (produced by `iconutil` and a standard PNG-in-ICO writer) but were not opened on Windows.
- The Discord and OpenClaw surfaces (C7–C9) were not exercised against live systems, by rule.
